Note: Section A (Prose, Poetry, Story, Khand Kavya — Hindi Literature, 50 marks) and Section B
(Sanskrit, Grammar, Essay, Letter Writing, Unseen passages — Language Skills, 50 marks) are
taught in parallel from April, so the subject stays varied and weak students can follow along and
retain it easily. The full syllabus is completed by October, leaving November and December
entirely for revision.
